If placeholder existed but key missing, out remains unchanged\n    return out, modified, missing\n\n\ndef strip_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove linesegarray-like elements within paragraph; return count removed.\"\"\"\n    removed=0\n    #
